# Cementitious-mortar fired-clay brick interior partition construction service

## 1. Scope and Applicability

This PCR describes construction of a non-loadbearing interior partition from fired-clay bricks and factory dry cementitious masonry premix, mixed with the actual supplied water at the work site. It begins with the physically accepted base/support interface and signed project design and ends with the unique finally accepted partition after the work package has completed its specified details, joint tooling, protection, setting, cleaning and actual corrections.

The design and supplier instructions for the actual project determine compatibility and required operations. The presence of a brick wall alone is insufficient to establish completion: every newly installed lintel, restraint, fixing, movement/slip/head detail and fire/acoustic closure required by that design belongs to the work package when it is its responsibility. Record which items already exist in the released interface and which are newly installed. A generic source that does not describe a particular detail does not remove that detail from the boundary.

This route excludes raw cement/sand/lime recipe mixing, lime-only mortar, brick or premix manufacture as foreground operations, structural base manufacture, loadbearing or exterior/cavity masonry, paint/plaster finishes, and service life or end-of-life beyond construction acceptance. Different products or starting conditions require a scope decision. Actual supplier/product instructions and project acceptance evidence govern the work; the cited general guidance supplies procedural context and no universal design certification or quantity factor.

## 3. Reference Flow

| Field | Value |
| --- | --- |
| What | A completed non-loadbearing fired-clay brick interior partition construction service |
| How much | 1 m2 of net unique finally accepted single-plane wall surface; openings excluded once; both faces not counted twice |
| How well | Meets the actual signed project design and supplier compatibility for wall geometry, support/restraint/slip/head details, lintels, movement/fire/acoustic closure, joint finish and acceptance; no universal fire/acoustic rating is inferred |
| How long or cycle | One actual construction lot from released entry through final acceptance; enumerate receipt, preparation, laying, protection/setting, cleaning/correction and acceptance periods with actual cutoffs, no fixed curing days or asset life |
| reference_flow_link | accepted_partition_service |

| Field | Value |
| --- | --- |
| reference_amount | 1 |
| reference_product_flow | Accepted interior brick partition construction service |
| reference_flow_property | Area `93a60a56-a3c8-19da-a746-0800200c9a66` |
| reference_unit_group | Units of area `93a60a57-a3c8-18da-a746-0800200c9a66` |
| reference_unit | m2 |
| required_qualifiers | site and geography; actual lot periods; wall/panel surface IDs and openings; thickness/bond/joint profile; non-loadbearing interior use; exact fired-clay SKU and moisture; factory cementitious premix grade/instructions; physical support and signed design release; actual restraint/lintel/movement/fire/acoustic detail schedule; temporary-support owner; actual protection/cure/cleaning conditions; final as-built acceptance; upstream dataset and packaging coverage |

A is the unnormalized positive area of the final accepted union. Actual corrections retain all inputs, energy, wastes and service availability in the lot numerator without adding another final wall area. Internal wet mortar uses actual kg and newly built uncured-wall handoffs use actual m2, neither fixed to the final reference amount. Existing-asset interface geometry/release/receipt remain independently measured m2 observations in own protocols and calculations, not product exchanges or production burdens. The final 1 m2 output is the calculated A/A identity for the same positive accepted A; it is not an assumed material yield.

## 4. Measurement and Unit Rules

| rule_id | Applies to | Required property | Required unit | Rule |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `measure_accepted_area` | final service | Area `93a60a56-a3c8-19da-a746-0800200c9a66` | m2 | Derive A from final accepted single-plane union geometry, subtract openings and unaccepted work once and require positive A. Normalize each physical numerator once; A/A is calculated 1. |
| `measure_interface_area` | existing base and wall handoffs | Area `93a60a56-a3c8-19da-a746-0800200c9a66` | m2 | Physical interface geometry is observational. Its documents are evidence. Neither enters kg mass reconciliation, and no new structural material is inferred. |
| `measure_material_bridge` | all material/waste/usable exits | Mass `93a60a56-a3c8-11da-a746-0800200b9a66` | kg | Preserve current moisture and state. Convert count/length/area only using actual same-SKU/state mass evidence; do not add a whole assembly and its constituent totals. |
| `measure_gross_stock` | external staged materials and supplied water | Mass `93a60a56-a3c8-11da-a746-0800200b9a66` | kg | Gross draw equals opening intact plus external receipts minus closing intact over all relevant custody locations. It includes usable exits; exclude closing intact once only and keep waste/WIP/exits exclusive. |
| `measure_water_state` | water-bearing mixes/walls/wastes | Mass `93a60a56-a3c8-11da-a746-0800200b9a66` | kg | Track total and bound/free water by actual state/time. Internal handoff water is separate from final retained water. Hydration retains bound water; missing closure is not vapour. |
| `measure_energy_lineage` | electricity | Energy | kWh | Use actual meter deltas with factor, interval and topology, and only the eligible master residual after all inside-master direct assignment once; exact dimensional support remains independently required. |
| `measure_service_unit` | access and support | Duration | h | Use actual contract/service availability and ownership periods. Do not substitute labour, a presumed shift or unsupported dimensions. |
| `measure_species_medium` | direct elementary outputs | Mass `93a60a56-a3c8-11da-a746-0800200b9a66` | kg | Identify actual outgoing substance, particulate basis and receiving medium, with capture distinction. No output uptake or generic dust substitution. |
| `count_unit_alias` | Count-based exchanges where present | Count | item | The PCR display alias item denotes the verified Number of items unit-group reference unit Item(s), factor 1. Preserve the actual reference-unit identity in a concrete dataset; this spelling change creates no mass, area or service equivalence. |

## 5. System Boundary

### Boundary Abstraction

| Field | Value |
| --- | --- |
| declared_starting_condition | Released existing physical base/support geometry with signed design and a resolved newly-installed-versus-existing component schedule |
| starting_condition_role | Received external physical quality/interface condition; geometry is observational, documents are evidence, neither is a new material purchase |
| product_classification_scope | The semantic interior non-loadbearing fired-clay brick construction service defined here, independent of external classification hierarchy |
| recursive_input_rule | A same-category purchased completed partition or inherited unresolved partly built wall is not silently unfolded into this route. Record its supplied state and upstream owner; require a scope review before changing the starting gate or duplicating its construction inventory. |
| upstream_dataset_requirement | Connect each actual external material/water/provider and waste route to an appropriate reviewed upstream dataset once. Internal material/feed and geometry handoffs carry existing ownership; document supplier packaging coverage and any separate residual packaging burdens. |
| disclosure | Publish exact route, entry/release and acceptance gates, actual component ownership and product instructions, period/custody/WIP boundaries, upstream dataset coverage, allocation decisions and unresolved quantity/identity/measurement gaps |

### Boundary Rules

| rule_id | Applies to | Rule | source_ids |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `boundary_entry` | entry_gate | Begin with an accepted physical base and released signed partition design. Require the exact brick/premix compatibility, geometry/openings, support loading, bottom/slip/head/lateral details, lintels, movement/fire/acoustic closures and temporary support ownership before work enters this route. An unresolved owner or compatibility is a gap. | `bda-workmanship-2023`; `ancon-restraint-interface` |
| `boundary_work` | all_four_nodes | Include receipt/storage losses, actual factory-premix mixing, laying/cutting, designed ancillary integration, joint tooling, protection, cementitious setting, cleaning, actual corrections and final acceptance. Generic guidance lacking a detail never permits its omission from the actual work package. | `bda-workmanship-2023`; `bda-mortar-2023` |
| `boundary_composition` | mix_mortar and cure_accept | The mix responsibility combines the exact factory dry cementitious premix and actual supplied water, handing over actual wet mortar. The cement-water reaction continues in its actual observed state through accepted wall handoff. Preserve bound/free water, unreacted/unused inputs, losses and off-spec states; infer neither a chemical pathway nor a yield or CO2 uptake. | `bda-mortar-2023` |
| `boundary_assembly` | lay_joint | Join actual fired-clay bricks, wet mortar and every required compatible ancillary to the released existing interface. The intended uncured wall and its constituent state pass to cure_accept; rejected assemblies, cuts and losses retain their own dispositions and producing-node responsibility. | `bda-workmanship-2023`; `ancon-restraint-interface` |
| `boundary_finishing` | lay_joint and cure_accept | Joint tooling is an explicit finishing responsibility within laying after a parent laid wall exists. Record parent state, actual tooling/closure inputs and finished uncured handoff. cure_accept owns later protection/cleaning and actual remedial finishing through accepted output, with residues and releases assigned to the producing node. No decorative coating or plaster is included by implication. | `bda-workmanship-2023` |
| `boundary_receipt` | staged_materials_and_packaging | One external gross receipt owner supplies each staged material and its carried packaging. Preserve own receipt rejection/usable exits and match real issue to downstream internal feed. Supplied water enters only its actual using node. Packaging upstream dataset coverage is counted once; waste, reusable exits and stock have material-specific owners. |  |
| `boundary_periods` | lot_and_shared_assets | Enumerate the actual receipt, mix, laying, protection/cure, cleaning/correction and acceptance periods, with cutoff stock, WIP, assets, replacement/termination events and every consumer linked to each period. Use actual boundaries and ownership; no assumed construction day, service life or annualization. |  |
| `boundary_exclusions` | external_interfaces | Exclude manufacture of bricks/premix and pre-existing base/structure from foreground operations but connect actual upstream datasets once at external material entry. Exclude lime-only/site raw-recipe mortars, loadbearing/exterior/cavity wall routes, painting/plaster, structural base manufacture and use/end-of-life beyond acceptance. A changed incoming state or newly owned excluded operation requires scope review. | `bda-workmanship-2023`; `bda-mortar-2023` |
| `boundary_atomic_completion` | all processes | Section 5 collection requirements are mandatory collection and applicability instructions, not aggregate inventory exchanges. Instantiate a distinct atomic flow card/exchange for every actual product, waste stream, substance and environmental compartment before completing a foreground dataset. Preserve every actual event, upstream burden, return and native unit; apply the linked formula to each compatible identity separately. Record environmental withdrawals as inputs and releases as outputs. Never sum unlike native units, substitute one UUID for a pool, infer zero from missing records, or duplicate an existing concrete card. Documentary retained-host interfaces carry state only and create no new purchased asset. Quantified completion is blocked until applicable members are resolved or evidenced absent; nonbinding screening aids do not supply missing amounts or chemistry. |  |

### Conditional inventory completion requirements

The following identifiers denote collection pools or documentary state ledgers. A pool may have zero, one or several actual members. Determine applicability from real records; retain the original quantity method and collection protocol for each member. Only compatible quantities may be reconciled together. Any old optional screening interval below remains a provisional aid for review and must not be imposed on a different material or on a mixed pool.

Process / prospective direction / type: `receive_setout` / `inputs` / `product`.

Account for actual wrapping, bags, liners, pallets and handling protection at receipt by composition and custody. This includes packaging still attached to materials issued later. Record whether its manufacture is already covered by each supplier dataset before adding any separate production burden.

It screens gross draw with losses and usable exits retained, not net installed material or a prescribed consumption rate. Exceedance calls for review and explanation, not rejection of valid measurements or clipping. This is not empirical evidence or a default inventory value.

Process / prospective direction / type: `receive_setout` / `outputs` / `product`.

Expand the actual schedule into zero, one or many exchanges, each with its real material/SKU, composition, current state, measured component kg, transfer event, sender/recipient and concrete independently verified UUID when resolved. #### Collection requirement: Materials actually issued to the cure and acceptance node (`issue_materials_cure_accept`)

Process / prospective direction / type: `receive_setout` / `outputs` / `product`.

Conditional unchanged-state Mass/kg dispatch from receive_setout to cure_accept, covering the actual material roles in component ledgers issue_protection_material, issue_cure_cleaning_material. Expand the actual schedule into zero, one or many exchanges, each with its real material/SKU, composition, current state, measured component kg, transfer event, sender/recipient and concrete independently verified UUID when resolved. The umbrella is not a fixed mixed product and the mass of several materials is not an identity. Retain each original component quantity calculation and match the same-SKU/current-state recipient input; no repeated purchase or new upstream production burden. Each instantiated exchange uses its own actual SKU/state/concrete-identity numerator/A; the separately named diagnostic role total is only a reconciliation view: do not publish an aggregate exchange in addition to its instantiated components. Unknown component identity/quantity or custody link remains a gap; no guessed UUID, automatic sum of quantities.

- Collection subject: Materials actually issued to the cure and acceptance node
- Flow property / unit: Mass / kg
- Amount rule: actual_issue_materials_cure_accept/A
- Value mode: `calculated_value`
- Specificity: `site_specific`
- Normalization basis: per reference flow
- Basis kind: `reference_flow`
- Evidence kind: `calculated_from_collection`
- Collection protocol: `cp_receive_setout`
- Sources: `bda-workmanship-2023`
- Unverified optional screening aid: Provisional individual planning screen
  - Range role: `qa_guardrail`
  - Lower: 0
  - Upper: 100
  - Unit: kg
  - Basis: per reference flow
  - Basis kind: `reference_flow`
  - Evidence kind: `reasoned_estimate`

An independently selected provisional 0..100 kg/m2 planning screen for actual receive_setout-to-cure_accept material dispatch. It is not the sum of former card bounds, a bill of materials, an empirical rate or a default amount. It permits review of actual lot procurement, detail density, intact stock and actual component dispatch without prescribing any SKU mix. Actual per-SKU/current-state component measurements and custody pairing control quantities; zero requires actual absence, unknown is not zero, and exceedance needs explanation rather than clipping. Apply this screen to each actual instantiated exchange numerator/A; the separately named diagnostic role total has no inventory Range and is not substituted for a component amount.

#### Collection requirement: Receipt packaging waste to custodian (`receive_packaging_waste`)

Process / prospective direction / type: `receive_setout` / `outputs` / `waste`.

Record packaging actually discarded while receiving and opening packs here. Separate polymer, paper, timber and metal destinations. Packaging carried onward is an issue, and an intact reusable pallet returned to its owner is a usable exit.

- Collection subject: Receipt packaging waste to custodian

  - Unit: kg
  - Basis: per reference flow

  - Evidence kind: `reasoned_estimate`

The 40 kg/m2 upper screen for receipt packaging waste to custodian is an independently chosen provisional planning trigger, allowing lot size, detail density and repeated actual work to vary. Only actual own receiving discard is included; downstream remedial removal stays at its actual producing node unless a real changed-state custody handoff is reviewed; the quantity still must reconcile to the matching material and water ledger. Zero is an allowable screening bound only; it never converts missing required evidence into nonoccurrence. Exceedance calls for review and explanation, not rejection of valid measurements or clipping. This is not empirical evidence or a default inventory value. Only material-resolved packaging physically opened/discarded during receipt belongs here; intact/reusable packaging returns and packaging discarded after an actual downstream issue have their own fates. No mortar or wall-removal mass is packaging waste. Actual rework or a custody return can affect this node only with independently evidenced real current-state transfer and reviewed compatible route/ownership; downstream removal stays with its actual producing node and is never reassigned merely by a waste name.

#### Collection requirement: Materials actually discarded during receipt (`receive_material_waste`)

Process / prospective direction / type: `receive_setout` / `outputs` / `waste`.

Conditional material-resolved receipt waste produced by receive_setout before actual downstream issue: fired-clay brick and actual dry premix, ancillary, protection or cleaning/remedial materials, in their measured real phase/current moisture/state. Expand each actual SKU/state/disposition/custodian/treatment destination into its own measured concrete waste exchange and independently verify its UUID only when actual identity evidence exists. Keep the original brick and non-brick component ledgers and their quantities in receipt material pools exactly once. Exclude packaging waste, usable supplier returns or external transfers, intact stock/WIP and downstream construction/remedial removals. A downstream removal stays with its actual producing node unless a real changed-state custody handoff has been reviewed; no such handoff is inferred. The umbrella is not one fixed mixed waste identity; each actual composition/state/custodian exchange has its own numerator/A, while a separately named diagnostic reconciliation view and its components are not separate additive exchanges. Unknown phase, quantity, identity or destination remains a gap.

- Collection subject: Materials actually discarded during receipt

Independently chosen provisional 0..600 kg/m2 screen for actual material discard owned by receipt. This is not a sum of the former brick and non-brick limits, an empirical reject fraction, a default waste amount or a permitted removal allowance. It screens only actual own receiving waste before issue, by measured SKU/state/phase and destination; downstream remedial removal remains at its producing node without a reviewed real changed-state custody handoff. Zero requires actual nonoccurrence evidence; unknown remains missing and a measured exceedance requires explanation, not clipping. Apply this screen to each actual instantiated exchange numerator/A; the separately named diagnostic role total has no inventory Range and is not substituted for a component amount. Only actual own receipt material discard before issue is in this role, using the real composition/SKU/current state/phase and custodian/treatment destination. Packaging, usable returns/transfers, stock/WIP and downstream wall removals remain separate. Actual rework or a custody return can affect this node only with independently evidenced real current-state transfer and reviewed compatible route/ownership; downstream removal stays with its actual producing node and is never reassigned merely by a waste name.

Exceedance calls for review and explanation, not rejection of valid measurements or clipping. This is not empirical evidence or a default inventory value. Only actual cutting/tooling/cleaning liquid or slurry collected at the laying operation belongs here, with current suspended solids/water composition and destination. Other-node wash liquid, solid discard and direct releases are separately owned. Actual rework or a custody return can affect this node only with independently evidenced real current-state transfer and reviewed compatible route/ownership; downstream removal stays with its actual producing node and is never reassigned merely by a waste name.

## 6. Process Inventory Structure

### Process Map

| process_id | process_name | inclusion | inclusion_condition | role | quantitative_reference |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `receive_setout` | Receive, survey and set out the designed partition | required | Actual scope route; conditional exchanges only when actual evidence establishes occurrence | Inspect the released base and project design before recording entry. Reconcile delivery tickets, SKU/state weighing, rejection and storage inspection. Log all intact stock at central stores and point-of-use sublocations, including unissued goods for future lots, with one custody owner. Document every actual material and carried-packaging dispatch and its recipient. Design paperwork proves readiness but is not a material. Maintain a complete installed-versus-existing component schedule. Existing interface observation roles base_design_interface and physical_interface_release preserve actual geometry/state and release/receipt responsibility, not product inventory. | same final A; internal handoffs retain actual quantities |
| `mix_mortar` | Prepare factory dry cementitious masonry mortar | required | Actual scope route; conditional exchanges only when actual evidence establishes occurrence | At every actual batch record exact dry premix ID, weighed received internal feed, actual supplied water, mixing operation and product instructions. Weigh wet dispatch and match laying receipt. Measure discarded mortar, wash liquid, packaging and usable exits separately. Characterize solids and water before setting; record reaction state changes, not an invented mix recipe or retarder time. | same final A; internal handoffs retain actual quantities |
| `lay_joint` | Lay, cut, restrain and finish brickwork joints | required | Actual scope route; conditional exchanges only when actual evidence establishes occurrence | At each panel/pass match actual brick, wet mortar, designed ancillary and packaging receipts. Record physical base interface release, geometry, cutting method, tooling, restraint and closure installation, water, waste and releases. Preserve cut/offcut and rejected assembly dispositions and all remedial events. Dispatch the actual uncured wall area with its constituent/water state to acceptance; no extra material purchase is created. Existing interface observation roles physical_interface_construction_feed preserve actual geometry/state and release/receipt responsibility, not product inventory. | same final A; internal handoffs retain actual quantities |
| `cure_accept` | Protect, cure, clean and accept the partition | required | Actual scope route; conditional exchanges only when actual evidence establishes occurrence | Follow actual protection/cure and cleaning procedures, recording conditions, temporary-support ownership, cleaning/repair goods and all actual correction events. Match the received uncured wall state and actual water pools. Collect as-built evidence for every required design detail and signed acceptance. Survey only finally accepted unique surfaces for A; leave unresolved work in identified WIP. | same final A; internal handoffs retain actual quantities |

The four responsibilities own the complete physical event ledger. Joint tooling is an explicit finishing responsibility within lay_joint followed by the actual tooled uncured-wall handoff. Cement-water setting is followed from mixing to the accepted state in cure_accept. Conditional water use, direct releases, packaging dispositions and remedial passes require occurrence evidence; unknown activation is a gap. No artificial extra node or material purchase is created by record subdivision.

Quarantined stock remains identified stock; a usable supplier return has a positive usable exit instead. Record cause, current moisture and actual waste custodian. |

Split actual material/formulation/SKU, current moisture/state, discarded phase and waste custodian/treatment destination. This conditional umbrella excludes brick-only discard, packaging discard, usable supplier returns and intact or processed stock/WIP. Do not move a receipt reject into mixing, laying or cure waste without its real issue/receipt handoff. Each actual material becomes its own measured exchange; an umbrella total and its components are never added together. Unknown composition, state, occurrence or destination remains a gap, not zero or an invented identity. |

### Process: Receive, survey and set out the designed partition (`receive_setout`)

Inspect the released base and project design before recording entry. Reconcile delivery tickets, SKU/state weighing, rejection and storage inspection. Log all intact stock at central stores and point-of-use sublocations, including unissued goods for future lots, with one custody owner. Document every actual material and carried-packaging dispatch and its recipient. Design paperwork proves readiness but is not a material. Maintain a complete installed-versus-existing component schedule.

#### Inputs

##### Product flows

###### Attributable electricity for receive, survey and set out the designed partition (`receive_setout_electricity`)

Measure the electricity attributable to this node, with meter serial, multiplier, interval and parent topology. Use direct deltas first. A master residual subtracts every inside-master directly assigned consumer exactly once; it never subtracts an outside-master direct load. Allocate only a positive residual over a complete positive eligible weight set. Negative or unknown residual blocks attribution and requires resolving reading/interval/topology/service evidence; never allocate, clip or convert it to a credit. Only a positive residual is allocated using a complete eligible consumer set and positive weight denominator. Exactly zero needs no weights after completeness is established.

- Selected flow: Attributable electricity for receive, survey and set out the designed partition
- Flow property / unit: Energy / kWh
- Amount rule: actual_receive_setout_electricity/A

### Process: Prepare factory dry cementitious masonry mortar (`mix_mortar`)

At every actual batch record exact dry premix ID, weighed received internal feed, actual supplied water, mixing operation and product instructions. Weigh wet dispatch and match laying receipt. Measure discarded mortar, wash liquid, packaging and usable exits separately. Characterize solids and water before setting; record reaction state changes, not an invented mix recipe or retarder time.

### Process: Lay, cut, restrain and finish brickwork joints (`lay_joint`)

##### Waste flows

###### Final cleaning water to custodian (`cure_wastewater`)

Measure aqueous cleaning residues delivered to a wastewater custodian, with water/solids partition and actual treatment route. A direct outgoing release has its own substance and medium evidence and is not also recorded as collected wastewater.

- Selected flow: Final cleaning water to custodian
- Flow property / unit: Mass / kg
- Amount rule: actual_cure_wastewater/A
- Value mode: `calculated_value`
- Specificity: `site_specific`
- Normalization basis: per reference flow
- Basis kind: `reference_flow`
- Evidence kind: `calculated_from_collection`
- Collection protocol: `cp_cure_accept`
- Sources: `bda-workmanship-2023`
- Range: Provisional individual planning screen
  - Range role: `qa_guardrail`
  - Lower: 0
  - Upper: 300
  - Unit: kg
  - Basis: per reference flow
  - Basis kind: `reference_flow`
  - Evidence kind: `reasoned_estimate`

The 300 kg/m2 upper screen for final cleaning water to custodian is an independently chosen provisional planning trigger, allowing lot size, detail density and repeated actual work to vary. The quantity still must reconcile to the matching material and water ledger. Zero is an allowable screening bound only; it never converts missing required evidence into nonoccurrence. Exceedance calls for review and explanation, not rejection of valid measurements or clipping. This is not empirical evidence or a default inventory value. Only actual aqueous cleaning/remedial liquid collected by this protection/acceptance operation belongs here, with measured water/solids/solute fractions and wastewater custodian. Other-node wash liquid, solid waste and direct liquid/vapour releases are not counted again. Actual rework or a custody return can affect this node only with independently evidenced real current-state transfer and reviewed compatible route/ownership; downstream removal stays with its actual producing node and is never reassigned merely by a waste name.

##### Elementary flows

###### Water vapour released during cure and final cleaning (`cure_water_vapour`)

Record measured or validated actual own-node H2O water vapour loss to air unspecified during protection, setting and cleaning; never infer evaporation from a residual gap. Water chemically bound in cementitious material remains retained mass. Determine actual water states rather than imposing a curing duration, hydration yield or default drying loss.

- Selected flow: water vapour `fe0acd60-3ddc-11dd-ac04-0050c2490048`
- Flow property / unit: Mass / kg
- Amount rule: actual_cure_water_vapour/A
- Value mode: `calculated_value`
- Specificity: `site_specific`
- Normalization basis: per reference flow
- Basis kind: `reference_flow`
- Evidence kind: `calculated_from_collection`
- Collection protocol: `cp_cure_accept`
- Sources: `bda-workmanship-2023`
- Range: Provisional individual planning screen
  - Range role: `qa_guardrail`
  - Lower: 0
  - Upper: 300
  - Unit: kg
  - Basis: per reference flow
  - Basis kind: `reference_flow`
  - Evidence kind: `reasoned_estimate`

The 300 kg/m2 upper screen for water vapour released during cure and final cleaning is an independently chosen provisional planning trigger, allowing lot size, detail density and repeated actual work to vary. The wide provisional amount screen cannot override the measured water input and exclusive ending-pool constraints. Zero is an allowable screening bound only; it never converts missing required evidence into nonoccurrence. Exceedance calls for review and explanation, not rejection of valid measurements or clipping. This is not empirical evidence or a default inventory value.

## 7. Allocation and Co-product Handling

The intended output is one accepted construction service. Internal interfaces, material transfers and original-unused usable exits do not create co-produced final services. Maintain separate material-resolved upstream and construction burden ledgers. Any net-use diagnostic is a reconciliation value only, with no second negative exchange or avoided-production claim:

| rule_id | Applies to | Rule | source_ids |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `allocation_subdivide` | own_node_materials | Use directly measured node/material/event quantities before allocation. Internal issues and feeds transfer quantities and existing burdens; they are not new purchases, co-products or additional final services. |  |
| `allocation_usable_exits` | positive_original_unused_exits | Keep gross receipt/draw quantities including usable original-unused exits, and record those exits positively by same grade/current state and recipient. Separate the original upstream material burden attributable to the exit when supported; retain all construction/rework burdens with the lot. A net-use diagnostic may subtract exits once for reconciliation only: it is not another exchange or negative credit. No avoided-production claim is created. |  |
| `allocation_power` | four_node_electricity | Apply direct converted meter deltas first. Calculate the actual master residual after subtracting every inside-master direct assignment once, excluding outside-master direct loads from subtraction. Zero residual needs no weights. Allocate a positive residual only over all eligible consumers with a positive denominator and causal evidence; reconcile aggregate assignments to actual master/direct totals once. Negative or unknown residual blocks attribution and requires resolving reading/interval/topology/service evidence; never allocate, clip or convert it to a credit. Only a positive residual is allocated using a complete eligible consumer set and positive weight denominator. Exactly zero needs no weights after completeness is established. |  |
| `allocation_service` | four_node_access_service | Identify every shared scaffold/hoist/extraction/protection asset, provider/service boundary and consuming node/period. Assign direct actual availability first, then only the residual of the same service interval using a complete eligible positive denominator and recorded causal basis. Actual owned idle and rework remain included. No labour proxy, presumed shift, whole asset repurchase or invented life applies. Negative or unknown residual blocks attribution and requires resolving reading/interval/topology/service evidence; never allocate, clip or convert it to a credit. Only a positive residual is allocated using a complete eligible consumer set and positive weight denominator. Exactly zero needs no weights after completeness is established. |  |
| `allocation_period` | all_event_periods | Carry cutoff intact stock and processed WIP with their exact custody and period links. Maintain recorded attribution decisions for shared assets, replacements and termination by period; close overlaps and gaps before finalization. The same purchase, meter delta, service interval or event cannot be assigned again in the next period. |  |
| `allocation_rework` | rejected_and_corrected_states | Retain every actual correction event and link any loop to its producing and receiving nodes. Distinguish usable original goods, rework material, discarded waste and unresolved WIP. Repeated physical work remains in the numerator; only unique finally accepted geometry enters A. A downgraded/recovered destination needs actual evidence and a reviewed burden decision, not an assumed credit. | `bda-workmanship-2023` |

## 8. Foreground Data Collection, Calculation, and Quality Rules

### Data Collection Protocols

Each protocol owns exactly one real node. Typed raw arrays or scalar amounts preserve native units, actual state, uncertainty, event and period grain. Node-prefixed common fields identify physical events, electronic revisions, material/SKU/state, sublocation/custody, design/instruction references, acceptance, correction parents and dispositions. Role-prefixed fields contain measured quantities or actual conversion evidence for inventory roles, explicit observational roles and component-ledger selectors; only the declared inventory roles create exchanges. Reading/service interval arrays retain serial or asset ID, start/end, source period, native unit, conversion factor, parent boundary, consumer and assignment evidence. Consumer-weight arrays enumerate every eligible consumer and its actual causal weight.

### Calculation Rules

A is produced by calc_accepted_area. Every actual_<row_id> is an unnormalized physical numerator in the row unit; q_<row_id> divides that numerator by A once. Each static card selector is its section 6 row_id; cp_ inputs identify own-node protocols. Provisional ranges never generate inventory quantities. Scalar outputs retain the units declared by their source cards and typed pool rules; area provenance never becomes a kg operand. Helper definitions below are also included in every parsed calculation rule that uses them.

| static_symbol | Definition |
| --- | --- |
| EVENT_SUM | Use the declared inventory-role or explicitly registered component-ledger selector and that role's own protocol raw fields to sum actual physical event amounts once after exact typed conversion. The ledger selector is not another inventory card or product identity. Duplicated electronic records collapse by physical event/revision; every real repeated work/correction event is retained. A component ledger is reconciled within its conditional umbrella, never also added as a separate umbrella exchange. |
| METER_ASSIGN | Convert actual reading deltas and assign direct energy plus only eligible master residual per topology and interval, requiring complete positive weights only for a positive residual. Negative or unknown residual blocks attribution and requires resolving reading/interval/topology/service evidence; never allocate, clip or convert it to a credit. Only a positive residual is allocated using a complete eligible consumer set and positive weight denominator. Exactly zero needs no weights after completeness is established. |
| SERVICE_ASSIGN | Assign actual contract-defined direct service availability plus only eligible same-boundary residual; reconcile every consumer and period once. Negative or unknown residual blocks attribution and requires resolving reading/interval/topology/service evidence; never allocate, clip or convert it to a credit. Only a positive residual is allocated using a complete eligible consumer set and positive weight denominator. Exactly zero needs no weights after completeness is established. |
| UNION_AREA | Area of unique accepted single-plane surface union minus openings and excluded/unaccepted polygons once, converted using actual survey unit evidence. |
| PORTION_MASS | Select only the recorded exclusive material/event/state portion of the named unnormalized source-card kg output. Portion records declare source_card_id, source_calc_output, physical_event_id, material/SKU, current state, period, measured portion kg or fraction with method/uncertainty, and exclusive destination. Partitioned portions must reconcile to the source output; unknown shares block closure. No area, composite-plus-constituents or already normalized value enters kg arithmetic. Each individual portion is nonnegative and cannot exceed its actual physical carrier; exclusive portions of one carrier together cannot exceed that carrier. Missing values are not zero. |
| WATER_PORTION | As PORTION_MASS, but return only measured water kg in the same source event/current state, identifying bound/free form, medium and supplied-versus-process-water origin. A wet waste total is not pure water. Multiple water pools partition the same source mass without overlap; unknown content remains a gap. Water portions and their exclusive sum cannot exceed the actual physical carrier. |
| WALL_COMPONENT_MASS | Return separately measured actual same-panel/current-state whole-wall mass OR measured constituent masses from the source area card bridge records, with event/panel/lot and paired handoff or final acceptance evidence. Require the whole-or-components partition and measurement bridge; never use area as a kg addend or an assumed density/recipe factor. Source area output identifies geometry only. |
| WIP_MASS | Sum actual opening/closing processed-stock observations by own card, source amount/event, material/SKU/state, period and custody. Records link prior closing stock or current retained event and distinguish intact unissued stock already removed in gross draw. Wall WIP requires the same measured whole-or-components kg bridge. Unknown quantities/ownership block closure. |
| WIP_WATER | Select measured water content of the exact processed WIP records used by WIP_MASS, by source event/current state/time, with bound/free and uncertainty. Do not include intact fresh closing stock, handoff, accepted retention or other exit pools again. |
| EVIDENCED_ZERO | Return zero only when the named own-node absence records demonstrate that no such actual pool exists for the full lot/period. Unknown, missing or nonzero records block closure and require the actual role and path to be reviewed, never conceal it in a balancing amount. |
| OBSERVED_GEOMETRY | Read the explicitly declared observational role and its own protocol raw geometry/native-unit/current-state records, preserving actual panel/surface IDs, independently measured m2, release/acceptance evidence and same-event sender/recipient responsibility. Match physical_interface_release to physical_interface_construction_feed by their real interface event and geometry/state. Unknown geometry or release status remains a blocking gate gap; documented nonoccurrence is not a presumed one. These are existing-asset observations only: no product exchange, supplier purchase, production burden, inventory Range or kg contribution is created. |
| EXCHANGE_COMPONENT_MASS | For one actual instantiated exchange identified by the recorded exchange key, select and sum only its measured events from the original component ledgers. The key includes actual material/SKU/composition, current state, physical_event_id or a documented same-SKU/state/concrete-identity aggregation of deduplicated physical events over the lot, concrete independently verified UUID when resolved, and actual custody/recipient; waste also includes phase, custodian and treatment destination. Reconcile selected event portions to their matching source component outputs, with nonnegative portions not exceeding the physical source carrier and no overlap. Return that one concrete exchange numerator in Mass/kg, never the mixed role total. Only actual kg component operands are eligible; length, area, count and existing-interface observations cannot be summed here. Any native-unit conversion must already have the measured same-SKU/current-state mass bridge. Expand the actual schedule into zero, one or many exchanges without inventing identity or quantity. Unknown key, component amount or custody/destination remains a gap. A separately named diagnostic role sum is for reconciliation only and is not another exchange. |

| rule_id | Applies to | Formula or rule | Inputs | Output | source_ids |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `calc_accepted_area` | cure_accept | A = UNION_AREA(cure_accept_accepted_surface_polygons, cure_accept_opening_polygons, cure_accept_excluded_surface_polygons, cure_accept_geometry_unit_factor) after cure_accept_final_acceptance_status and cure_accept_gate_component_completeness_ref confirm the gate. Union accepted single-plane surface polygons, exclude opening and unaccepted surface polygons once, and convert to m2 using the actual survey scale. A must be positive. Count repeated forms or remedial passes once in geometry but retain their physical burdens. UNION_AREA definition: Area of unique accepted single-plane surface union minus openings and excluded/unaccepted polygons once, converted using actual survey unit evidence. | `cure_accept_accepted_surface_polygons`; `cure_accept_opening_polygons`; `cure_accept_excluded_surface_polygons`; `cure_accept_geometry_unit_factor`; `cure_accept_final_acceptance_status`; `cure_accept_gate_component_completeness_ref`; `cp_cure_accept`; `UNION_AREA` | `A` |  |
| `calc_base_design_interface` | receive_setout | actual_base_design_interface = OBSERVED_GEOMETRY(base_design_interface, cp_receive_setout); q_base_design_interface = actual_base_design_interface/A. This own-node observational calculation preserves all original measured geometry/native-unit/current-state fields, surface/panel IDs, actual interface event/recipient links and release/acceptance evidence. It is not an inventory flow, material purchase or production handoff burden. Area remains m2; never use it in kg closure. The observed quantity is actual, not reference1; unknown state/geometry blocks the design gate. OBSERVED_GEOMETRY definition: Read the explicitly declared observational role and its own protocol raw geometry/native-unit/current-state records, preserving actual panel/surface IDs, independently measured m2, release/acceptance evidence and same-event sender/recipient responsibility. Match physical_interface_release to physical_interface_construction_feed by their real interface event and geometry/state. Unknown geometry or release status remains a blocking gate gap; documented nonoccurrence is not a presumed one. These are existing-asset observations only: no product exchange, supplier purchase, production burden, inventory Range or kg contribution is created. | `base_design_interface_actual_quantity`; `base_design_interface_native_unit`; `base_design_interface_mass_or_geometry_bridge`; `base_design_interface_actual_state`; `base_design_interface_measured_absence_evidence`; `base_design_interface_destination`; `cp_receive_setout`; `A`; `base_design_interface`; `OBSERVED_GEOMETRY` | `actual_base_design_interface`; `q_base_design_interface` | `bda-workmanship-2023` |

Own-node full-period absence evidence; any actual nonzero or unknown other_exclusive_transfer blocks closure and needs explicit role/path review. All pool assignments preserve the same source event/material/state/period and reconcile to actual source outputs. A source whole amount and its components use one basis only. Internal mass uses source handoff records; geometry is excluded. Original-unused positive exits remain in gross inputs and have no negative credit. Actual gross product mass excludes separately counted packaging using a measured constituent split; never add a packaged whole and its packaging again. WALL_COMPONENT_MASS definition: Return separately measured actual same-panel/current-state whole-wall mass OR measured constituent masses from the source area card bridge records, with event/panel/lot and paired handoff or final acceptance evidence. Require the whole-or-components partition and measurement bridge; never use area as a kg addend or an assumed density/recipe factor. Source area output identifies geometry only. WIP_MASS definition: Sum actual opening/closing processed-stock observations by own card, source amount/event, material/SKU/state, period and custody. Records link prior closing stock or current retained event and distinguish intact unissued stock already removed in gross draw. Wall WIP requires the same measured whole-or-components kg bridge. Unknown quantities/ownership block closure. EVIDENCED_ZERO definition: Return zero only when the named own-node absence records demonstrate that no such actual pool exists for the full lot/period. WIP_WATER definition: Select measured water content of the exact processed WIP records used by WIP_MASS, by source event/current state/time, with bound/free and uncertainty. Unknown, missing or nonzero records block closure and require the actual role and path to be reviewed, never conceal it in a balancing amount. PORTION_MASS definition: Select only the recorded exclusive material/event/state portion of the named unnormalized source-card kg output. Portion records declare source_card_id, source_calc_output, physical_event_id, material/SKU, current state, period, measured portion kg or fraction with method/uncertainty, and exclusive destination. Partitioned portions must reconcile to the source output; unknown shares block closure. No area, composite-plus-constituents or already normalized value enters kg arithmetic. Each individual portion is nonnegative and cannot exceed its actual physical carrier; exclusive portions of one carrier together cannot exceed that carrier. Missing values are not zero. WIP_MASS definition: Sum actual opening/closing processed-stock observations by own card, source amount/event, material/SKU/state, period and custody. Records link prior closing stock or current retained event and distinguish intact unissued stock already removed in gross draw. Wall WIP requires the same measured whole-or-components kg bridge. Unknown water content blocks closure.; cure_water_vapour_material_bound_and_free_entry_water_kg = WATER_PORTION(actual_protection_material, cure_accept_material_entry_water_portion_records) + WATER_PORTION(actual_cure_cleaning_material, cure_accept_material_entry_water_portion_records) + WATER_PORTION(actual_cure_accept_packaging_feed, cure_accept_material_entry_water_portion_records). Each source card/output/event and actual state/time is retained in the portion record. Only inherent/attached water in these otherwise received materials, including actual premix moisture or formulated solution water. Excludes fresh supplied water and the wet process handoff; factory water already in a product is not another fresh-water purchase. Measured water kg or a measured same-state water fraction is required; a total wet mass is not water mass. Bound/free pools, source whole OR constituents and other fates are exclusive. Unknown water content blocks closure.; cure_water_vapour_opening_processed_water_kg = WIP_WATER(cure_accept_opening_processed_wip_records, cure_accept_opening_processed_wip_water_records). Water portion of the exact opening processed WIP observations, linked by own source card/output/event and same current-state measurement. Exclude intact unissued/fresh closing stock and all handoff, accepted-retention and waste/exit pools. Opening WIP water links the prior cutoff closing state rather than a new entry.; cure_water_vapour_closing_processed_wip_water_kg = WIP_WATER(cure_accept_closing_processed_wip_records, cure_accept_closing_processed_wip_water_records). Water portion of the exact closing processed WIP observations, linked by own source card/output/event and same current-state measurement. Exclude intact unissued/fresh closing stock and all handoff, accepted-retention and waste/exit pools. Opening WIP water links the prior cutoff closing state rather than a new entry.; cure_water_vapour_next_handoff_water_kg = EVIDENCED_ZERO(cure_accept_next_handoff_water_kg_absence_records). Own-node full-period absence evidence; any actual nonzero or unknown next_handoff_water_kg blocks closure and needs explicit role/path review.; cure_water_vapour_final_retained_bound_water_kg = WATER_PORTION(WALL_COMPONENT_MASS(accepted_partition_service_measured_component_mass_records, accepted_partition_service_same_state_mass_bridge_ref, accepted_partition_service_whole_or_components_partition_ref), cure_accept_final_retained_bound_water_kg_water_portion_records). Each source card/output/event and actual state/time is retained in the portion record. Only measured bound water retained in the final accepted wall state; no default stoichiometry or hydration yield and no duplicate customer handover. Measured water kg or a measured same-state water fraction is required; a total wet mass is not water mass. Bound/free pools, source whole OR constituents and other fates are exclusive. Unknown water content blocks closure.; cure_water_vapour_final_retained_free_water_kg = WATER_PORTION(WALL_COMPONENT_MASS(accepted_partition_service_measured_component_mass_records, accepted_partition_service_same_state_mass_bridge_ref, accepted_partition_service_whole_or_components_partition_ref), cure_accept_final_retained_free_water_kg_water_portion_records). Each source card/output/event and actual state/time is retained in the portion record. Only measured free water retained in the final accepted wall state, exclusive of bound water and all other ending pools. Measured water kg or a measured same-state water fraction is required; a total wet mass is not water mass. Bound/free pools, source whole OR constituents and other fates are exclusive. Unknown water content blocks closure.; cure_water_vapour_solid_waste_water_kg = WATER_PORTION(actual_cure_solid_waste, cure_accept_solid_waste_water_kg_water_portion_records). Each source card/output/event and actual state/time is retained in the portion record. Only measured water within material discards/captured residues; material-resolved source waste retains current moisture, not a total-as-water assumption. Measured water kg or a measured same-state water fraction is required; a total wet mass is not water mass. Bound/free pools, source whole OR constituents and other fates are exclusive. Unknown water content blocks closure.; cure_water_vapour_aqueous_waste_water_kg = WATER_PORTION(actual_cure_wastewater, cure_accept_aqueous_waste_water_kg_water_portion_records). Each source card/output/event and actual state/time is retained in the portion record. Only actual water portion of collected wastewater, excluding suspended solids and other solutes; no duplicate direct release. Measured water kg or a measured same-state water fraction is required; a total wet mass is not water mass. Bound/free pools, source whole OR constituents and other fates are exclusive. Unknown water content blocks closure.; cure_water_vapour_direct_liquid_release_water_kg = WATER_PORTION(actual_cure_other_release, cure_accept_direct_liquid_release_water_kg_water_portion_records). Each source card/output/event and actual state/time is retained in the portion record. Only actually released outgoing liquid H2O component in an evidenced receiving medium, exclusive of vapour, other substances and collected wastewater. Measured water kg or a measured same-state water fraction is required; a total wet mass is not water mass. Bound/free pools, source whole OR constituents and other fates are exclusive. Unknown water content blocks closure.; cure_water_vapour_other_exclusive_water_exit_kg = EVIDENCED_ZERO(cure_accept_other_exclusive_water_exit_absence_records). Own-node full-period absence evidence; any actual nonzero or unknown other_exclusive_water_exit blocks closure and needs explicit role/path review. All water entries and endings are exclusive by actual source material/event/state/time and measured portion lineage. Fresh gross equals the own supplied-water card numerator, with intact fresh closing stock already excluded once. No fresh stock subtraction appears again. WATER_PORTION definition: As PORTION_MASS, but return only measured water kg in the same source event/current state, identifying bound/free form, medium and supplied-versus-process-water origin. A wet waste total is not pure water. Multiple water pools partition the same source mass without overlap; unknown content remains a gap. Water portions and their exclusive sum cannot exceed the actual physical carrier. WALL_COMPONENT_MASS definition: Return separately measured actual same-panel/current-state whole-wall mass OR measured constituent masses from the source area card bridge records, with event/panel/lot and paired handoff or final acceptance evidence. Require the whole-or-components partition and measurement bridge; never use area as a kg addend or an assumed density/recipe factor. Source area output identifies geometry only. WIP_WATER definition: Select measured water content of the exact processed WIP records used by WIP_MASS, by source event/current state/time, with bound/free and uncertainty. Do not include intact fresh closing stock, handoff, accepted retention or other exit pools again. EVIDENCED_ZERO definition: Return zero only when the named own-node absence records demonstrate that no such actual pool exists for the full lot/period. Unknown, missing or nonzero records block closure and require the actual role and path to be reviewed, never conceal it in a balancing amount. PORTION_MASS definition: Select only the recorded exclusive material/event/state portion of the named unnormalized source-card kg output. Portion records declare source_card_id, source_calc_output, physical_event_id, material/SKU, current state, period, measured portion kg or fraction with method/uncertainty, and exclusive destination. Partitioned portions must reconcile to the source output; unknown shares block closure. No area, composite-plus-constituents or already normalized value enters kg arithmetic. Each individual portion is nonnegative and cannot exceed its actual physical carrier; exclusive portions of one carrier together cannot exceed that carrier. Missing values are not zero. WIP_MASS definition: Sum actual opening/closing processed-stock observations by own card, source amount/event, material/SKU/state, period and custody. Records link prior closing stock or current retained event and distinguish intact unissued stock already removed in gross draw. Wall WIP requires the same measured whole-or-components kg bridge. Unknown quantities/ownership block closure. | `actual_cure_water`; `actual_cure_unused_return`; `cure_accept_unused_fresh_exit_water_portion_records`; `WATER_PORTION`; `actual_cure_wall_feed`; `cure_wall_feed_measured_component_mass_records`; For each material/SKU/state, reconcile gross draw against actual issues, own receipt discards, positive usable exits and exclusive processed retention/WIP/other transfers. Pool totals must derive from exact own-card unnormalized outputs or measured own-state observations with physical-event/portion lineage; manually entered summaries cannot replace that lineage. Match actual custody-event issue/feed pairs. Unissued future-lot intact goods stay in receipt stock at every sublocation. Supplier packaging already covered upstream receives no second manufacturing burden, while its physical custody/waste remains visible. For each water-bearing node, process-handoff water and separately characterized entry-material moisture are exclusive origins. Ending pools separate handoff, final bound/free retention, material/wastewater fractions, actual liquid releases, original-unused fresh exits, WIP, other evidenced exits and validated vapour. An intermediate handoff is not also final retention. Gross fresh draw already excludes intact fresh closing stock once. Bound water remains retained; actual evaporation requires independent evidence and unknown gaps never become releases.

Unknown water content blocks closure. |

### Data Quality Requirements

| requirement_id | Applies to | Requirement | Evidence |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `dq_design` | entry and final acceptance | Retain signed design, exact supplier compatibility and current project instructions for every required component and the as-built acceptance schedule. | design_release_ref; component_schedule_ref; supplier_instruction_ref; acceptance_ref |
| `dq_stock` | all material and packaging ledgers | Document every material/state/custody sublocation and exclusive final fate, including point-of-use future stock; join each gross draw, issue, feed, exit and WIP quantity to actual events. | stock_custody_partition_ref; custody evidence; scale records; upstream_coverage_ref |
| `dq_water` | each water-bearing node | Distinguish the water content of incoming/handed-over/retained/waste states at their actual time. Bound water remains mass; unexplained closure is uncertainty. | pool_partition_ref; moisture methods; evaporation_method_ref; uncertainty_kg |
| `dq_meter` | each energy or shared-service node | Keep actual serial, factors, source intervals, topology and the full eligible consumer set. Reconcile direct and residual assignments; prove the zero-residual case. | reading interval arrays; conversion_factor_ref; meter_topology_ref; contract_unit_ref |
| `dq_event` | all real occurrences | Deduplicate electronic revisions using physical event lineage, while preserving real repeated work and its node/period. Unresolved replacement/termination remains a gap. | physical_event_id; record_revision; rework_parent_event_id; period_attribution_ref |
| `dq_quantity` | all published amounts | Keep original records and bridge uncertainty, with unnormalized quantities and one A normalization. Replace provisional screens only using reviewed independent evidence; never validate a sample by its own maximum. | calibration; native quantities; actual same-state bridge; calculation audit |
| `dq_identity` | all resolved and unresolved exchanges | Resolve actual material/provider/species/medium and independent dimensional supports before claiming final downstream exchange readiness. A usable quantity does not prove database identity. | actual supplier/material and waste destination evidence; verified identity/support decisions |

## 9. Validation Rules

Validation evaluates declared route, ownership and evidence, preserving performed/skipped checks and uncertainty. Missing identities or quantities stay disclosed in their actual roles, never become zero or generic substitutes. A mathematical or structural check cannot alone certify physical design, external flow identities or publication readiness:

| rule_id | Applies to | Rule | source_ids |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `validation_gate` | scope_and_final_output | Confirm released entry design and final as-built completeness for all actual compatible restraints, lintels, movement/fire/acoustic closures and protection/cure/cleaning. Exclude unresolved panels from accepted A and retain their WIP/correction evidence. | `bda-workmanship-2023`; `ancon-restraint-interface` |
| `validation_geometry` | area_and_mass | Require A > 0 from the union of finally accepted single-plane surfaces with openings excluded once. Neither two faces, repeated acceptance forms nor rework passes multiply A. Physical interface area is observational and cannot enter a kg balance or imply newly supplied structure. |  |
| `validation_material` | gross_stock_and_transfers | Reconcile each material/SKU/state ledger from opening and external receipts through all-location intact closing stock, actual issues, own receipt waste/usable exits and exclusive WIP. Pair issue/feed by event, quantity, state and custody. All unissued future-lot stock remains in receipt custody wherever stored; no repeated purchase or second stock subtraction. |  |
| `validation_units` | conversion_and_measured_absence | Require exact same-SKU/current-state count/length/area-to-kg bridges, calibrated units and explicit composition/whole-versus-component ownership. Never sum mixed dimensions. Prove nonoccurrence before zero; missing records and conditional activation uncertainty remain gaps. |  |
| `validation_water` | mix_lay_cure | Check own-node time/state-exclusive water pools against actual gross supplied water and attached incoming water. Remove fresh closing intact once only. Separate actual handoff, final bound/free retention, wastes, liquid releases, usable exits and processed WIP. H2O to air unspecified needs actual validated evaporation; a residual or hydration state change is insufficient. | `bda-mortar-2023` |
| `validation_release` | elementary_output_roles | Require actual outgoing substance/particle basis and receiving medium for direct releases; prevent captured-dust/waste duplication. Output release cards cannot represent uptake. Any evidenced uptake needs its own reviewed input semantics; no default carbonation or universal dust identity. |  |
| `validation_meter` | meter_topology_and_service | Audit meter reading serial, source interval, factors, parent topology and complete eligible consumer set; check residual arithmetic, inside/outside boundary, zero-residual exception and aggregate reconciliation. Service h needs actual provider/contract unit and period, never an assumed shift or labour proxy. Negative or unknown residual blocks attribution and requires resolving reading/interval/topology/service evidence; never allocate, clip or convert it to a credit. Only a positive residual is allocated using a complete eligible consumer set and positive weight denominator. Exactly zero needs no weights after completeness is established. |  |
| `validation_rework` | all_reject_routes | Each rejection has an actual state, owner and path: rework, documented usable exit, recovery/downgrade with reviewed burden treatment, waste or retained WIP. Match loop events and preserve all actual corrections. An unresolved rejected surface does not become final accepted output. | `bda-workmanship-2023` |
| `validation_periods` | period_asset_event_linkage | Enumerate all lot phases and periods and link inputs, assets, outputs and correction/replacement/termination events. Review period-specific attribution and prevent duplicate carryover of burdens or accepted geometry. Unresolved replacement or termination ownership blocks finalization. |  |
| `validation_quality` | ranges_and_identity | Treat all provisional reasoned_estimate ranges as review screens only. They are not empirical factors, acceptance limits or permission to use a missing value. Verify actual exchange identity and property/unit separately from quantity evidence; unresolved provider/material/species and Duration/Energy support remain explicit review gaps. |  |

## 10. Published Dataset Profile

| Field | Value |
| --- | --- |
| dataset_role | Foreground construction-lot data package, with downstream process or lifecyclemodel projections |
| downstream_use | secondary_dataset and/or background_dataset only within the declared accepted interior partition route and qualifiers |
| allowed_use | Construction-stage modelling of the exact defined service, using actual upstream materials, providers and destinations with reviewed scope and measurements |
| excluded_use | Universal wall design/certification; material manufacturing inventory; exterior/loadbearing/cavity/lime-only/site-recipe routes; decorative finish or building use/end-of-life inventory; assumed lifetime/curing/weather/recipe defaults |
| required_metadata | PCR identity/version; geography/site/lot and actual phase/period boundaries; brick/premix/ancillary IDs and state; component ownership/design/instruction/asbuilt acceptance; unique area and opening geometry; all stock/custody/WIP and rework event lineage; upstream dataset/packaging coverage; energy/service consumer topology and allocation; measured uncertainty |
| required_quality_disclosure | Measured/calculated quantities versus provisional screens; performed/skipped checks; identity/property/unit gaps; source age/scope limits; material/water closure and missing records; residual allocation evidence; reject/rework dispositions and unresolved period/ownership decisions |
| update_trigger | Actual product/route/design ownership, supplier instructions, accepted gate or upstream dataset boundary changes; materially revised measurements/period attribution; new supported identities or independent quantitative evidence |

## 11. Data Sources

The BDA manuals describe general clay-brick workmanship and mortar practice, including historical examples and multiple routes. They do not establish factory-premix consumption, brick dimensions, laying height, cure duration, a wetting instruction or current project weather thresholds. Mortar volume percentages, tonnes per brick counts, component ratios and retarder examples are not factors for this PCR. The restraint article explains an engineering interface; its blockwork examples do not certify every fired-clay brick. Actual project design and product instructions remain required foreground evidence.

| source_id | type | reference | used_for |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| bda-workmanship-2023 | handbook | Brick Development Association, Good Site Practice and Workmanship, April 2023, 19 pages. https://d23z90m210izbx.cloudfront.net/uploads/downloads/13.-Good-Site-Practice-Workmanship-General-Guide-2023.f1678702110.pdf | pp5-8 design, reference acceptance, delivery/storage/handling; pp9-11 setout, full joints, cutting and timing/tooling; pp14-15 actual protection/weather context. Generic procedure only; project/supplier details control. |
| bda-mortar-2023 | handbook | Brick Development Association, Mortar for Brickwork, April 2023, 18 pages. https://d23z90m210izbx.cloudfront.net/uploads/downloads/08.-Mortar-for-Brickwork-Technical-Guide-2023.f1678701358.pdf | pp5-6 function and design selection; pp8-10 water/suction, actual mixing and waste; pp12-14 cementitious factory dry/silo states; p15 lime-only alternatives outside this route. No universal numeric conversion or curing factor. |
| ancon-restraint-interface | extension_guidance | Ancon, What is a Head Restraint and why are Head Restraints required in masonry panels?, 8 May 2017. https://www.ancon.co.uk/blog/what-is-a-head-restraint-and-why-are-head-restraints-required-in-masonry-panels | Designer-controlled top restraint and movement interface, compatibility and omission consequences only; no article dimensions/spacing/load factors are imported into this PCR. |
